## IR-563 — Truthful research metadata

[ADR-046](../adr/046-declared-research-origin-and-publication-metadata.md) records the user-approved contract. Origin is explicitly declared and never grants access. Existing records migrate to unknown without author or publication-year inference. Missing-author labels replace institutional and submitting-owner substitutions; reference/synthetic badges and neutral catalogue wording prevent false institutional claims. Publication year is separate from completion and added dates. Authorized editors/importers declare metadata; the bounded administrative correction path is documented in [RESEARCH_METADATA.md](../engineering/RESEARCH_METADATA.md).

| Requirement | Verification boundary |
|---|---|
| Declared origin and separate publication year round-trip; invalid origin rejected | `records/test_research_metadata.py` write/list serializers |
| Origin does not widen editing permissions | `records/test_research_metadata.py` authenticated HTTP PATCH |
| Explicit spreadsheet metadata survives import; invalid origin is reported | `records/test_excel_import.py` real XLSX through HTTP |
| Existing rows preserve authors, owners, completion and workflow | `records/test_research_metadata_migration.py` historical populated schema |
| Administrative correction is authorized, atomic, evidence-backed and audited; dry-run writes nothing | `records/test_correct_research_metadata.py` management command |
| Ask IRIS carries real authors, declared origin and publication year | `ai/tests/test_ask_http.py` answer HTTP response |
| Honest missing-author state, origin badges, verified reference and date distinctions | Discover, SourceContextPanel, PaperViewPage and EditDetailsDialog rendered-page tests |
| Authorless citations and no completion/creation-year publication fallback | `discover/researchMetadata.test.ts` public citation formatter |

The verified external fixture is [Attention Is All You Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/1706.03762), checked against arXiv on 2026-10-30: all eight authors and original publication year 2017. The test deliberately uses a different completion year. An existing source-card assertion was deliberately corrected: its fixture records no authors, so a nonempty invented byline is no longer required.

Verification on 2026-10-30: local frontend full suite **105 files / 1,323 tests passed**; TypeScript passed. The first frontend full run caught the new audit event missing from the frontend filter; that mapping was added and the full run repeated successfully. A copy of the populated local database was migrated across records 0020 → 0021: **20 records, 14 author rows, 20 ownership rows**, with identical before/after hashes of every pre-existing column. All origins became unknown and publication years remained null. Backend focused checks passed 31 tests; the corrected historical migration fixture passed separately. Initial backend attempts interrupted by PostgreSQL recovery are not counted as verification. The broad local backend run reported failures and was stopped after clean CI completed; it is not a passing full-suite claim.

[PR #348](https://github.com/moisturicer/IRIS/pull/348), implementation commit `0757adc`: [clean CI run 40990153412](https://github.com/moisturicer/IRIS/actions/runs/40990153412) passed **Backend, Frontend, Security and Docker Compose**. Backend: **4,556 passed, 1 expected xfail, 415 subtests passed**; the authorization matrix executed **40 tests**. Frontend: **106 files / 1,335 tests passed**, plus lint/build. Migration consistency and backend static checks passed. Agent review against `b2c5187`: Standards **0 hard violations, 1 nonblocking duplicated-renderer concern**; Spec **0 findings**. Human approval and merge remain outstanding.

The interrupted broad local run included `No active EmbeddingSpace is configured` errors in its reused test database; it did not establish a code regression. The clean CI database executed the full backend suite successfully. Task-owned test databases and temporary source copies may be removed after verification; no live metadata corrections were executed.

## IR-646 and IR-648 — Account locks and password changes end sessions

Locking an account ends all its open tracked sessions with reason `locked` and blacklists its outstanding refresh tokens. Unlocking never revives those sessions. Both JWT authentication and refresh apply the same active-and-unlocked account rule, including direct database locks, deactivation, and access tokens without `sid`.

A successful password change ends every other tracked session with reason `password_changed`, preserving the request's session and its refresh token. Without `sid`, every tracked session ends. A rejected change does not end sessions or emit an audit event. `PASSWORD_CHANGED` records the actor with empty metadata; its choice-only migration is `audit/0009_password_changed` and the frontend audit filter includes it.

| Guarantee | Public verification boundary |
| --- | --- |
| Lock, unlock, direct lock, deactivation, no-sid access, session list and unchanged lock audit events | `accounts/test_session_invalidation.py::LockingEndsSessionsTests`: real sign-in, authenticated HTTP request, refresh, administrative API and audit API |
| Current device survives; other device ends; new/old passwords; rejected change; no sid; rotation; older stored JWT payloads | `accounts/test_session_invalidation.py::PasswordChangeEndsOtherSessionsTests`: password API, request, refresh, sign-in and audit API |
| Distinct account-locked and password-changed-elsewhere sign-out messages, with axe checks | `features/auth/LoginPage.test.tsx`: rendered login alert queried by accessible role |
| New audit event is available in the audit filter | `features/audit/AuditLogPage.test.tsx`: rendered filter options compared with the backend vocabulary |

Compatibility: before this change, `OutstandingToken.token` was stored before `sid` was added to the issued token. New sign-ins store the complete payload. During a password change, older stored payloads without a session identity are not individually blacklisted because the current device cannot be identified safely from them; every issued refresh still carries its session identity and the shared session rule refuses ended sessions. Account locking blacklists all outstanding tokens, including these older records.

Local focused verification on 2026-10-30: backend session invalidation, existing session, password-policy and enum-vocabulary suites passed. TypeScript passed; focused frontend suites passed **46 tests**. Both security regressions were observed before the fixes: a locked no-sid access token returned 200, and another device's access token returned 200 after a password change. The strengthened password-changed-elsewhere UI assertion also failed before the message changed. Full-suite, migration-copy and CI evidence are recorded in the PR when complete. Human review and approval remain required.
### IR-533 / IR-538 / IR-207 / IR-555 — entry-flow accessibility and truthful lock reporting

**Requirements:** NFR-A1 (WCAG 2.1 AA), NFR-U3 (readable, consistent entry controls), NFR-S6 (account protection). The project lead confirmed the four-screen scope, remaining lock dialog, removal of unsupported statistics, and automated plus manual review evidence on 2026-10-30. The 44×44 and disabled-text contrast criteria are stricter product requirements, not blanket AA rules. The authority for entry accessibility remains `docs/ui-ux/12-accessibility.md`; IR-533 and its children identify the implementation work.

Verification now uses `AuthLayout` and the shared `ButtonLink`, announces its result and failure reason in one polite status region, and explains RDCO review after email verification. No inline verification brand panel or invented quotation remains. Registration's confirmation also adopts `ButtonLink`. `entryTokens.test.ts` covers verification. Full-width buttons keep disabled text readable; entry navigation, consent labels and alert dismiss controls meet the 44×44 target. The gold text/fill lint guard runs across production TypeScript sources, preserves gold borders/rings, and documents the decorative library-star exception.

Axes now receives Django's underlying request and reads the supplied username credential, so its middleware can report the actual lock. Its JSON 429 names `too_many_attempts` and computes `unlock_at` from axes' stored failure windows, including attempts that extend the lock and the ASGI callback. A valid password on a manually locked account gets 403 `locked_by_admin` without a promised unlock time. The frontend prioritizes these codes, retains the older plain-text fallback without a timer, and keeps the existing tab-local counter for its own lock. The dialog uses shared focus containment/Escape/return behavior and an explicit sign-in return target because that control is disabled while the dialog opens. The configured server cool-off remains unchanged.

**Executed evidence:** 191 entry/primitives/guard tests; 2 added dialog keyboard/axe tests; the final three-file sign-in/signup/dialog regression pass had 71 tests. All 64 accounts REST tests passed on isolated PostgreSQL, including six timed/manual/ASGI lock tests and the existing current-user role-request seam. Backend static checks, frontend lint (six existing warnings), TypeScript and production build passed. Deliberate `text-gold hover:bg-gold-dark` reintroduction made lint fail, then was removed. Browser audit: 36 combinations across 360/768/1280/1745px, no reported WCAG A/AA violation, horizontal overflow or measured target below 44×44; minimum computed text-pair contrast 4.59:1. See [the evidence, screenshots, incomplete contrast results and human-review checklist](evidence/IR-533/README.md). Automated checks do not certify full conformance.

**Review gate:** actual screen-reader and browser-zoom verification and named human approval remain required. IR-203 and IR-209 remain open until the consolidated work is approved and merged. No Done status or full WCAG conformance is claimed here.

## IR-608 — ADR-042 safety and recovery evidence

- A11: `evaluation/tests/test_scenario_http.py` uses the authenticated IR-606 collector, private records and between-turn revocation. Public history withholds prose derived from a revoked cited Record. Foreign Conversation access remains 404.
- A09: the collector exercises candidate-only search, empty search, rejected tool arguments and answer failure after authorized evidence; private observations remain distinct from safe public output.
- A12: `test_durable_processes.py` launches independent interpreters over real shared Postgres for cancel, crash/redelivery, stale attempts and revision races; HTTP reconnect and state tests run separately. Scripted providers establish correctness, not throughput or broker behavior.
- A13: public HTTP projection suites are separate from the named manual browser exercise in `docs/evaluation/general-tools/scenarios.md`. Browser surfaces without evidence remain not_measured.
- A15: `test_controller_security_http.py` exercises malicious paper instructions and disabled public research without widening the tool registry or Paper Chat scope.

`eval_scenarios --workers` writes measured/not_measured results, distinguishes provider/worker/browser evidence and blocks enablement on failures or missing exercises. `evaluation/tests/test_scenarios.py` verifies that skipped/absent checks never pass and diagnostics never enter the report. See the IR-608 PR for executed results and remaining browser measurements.

IR-608 CI security follow-up: scenario JUnit XML uses `defusedxml`; `test_xml_entities_are_rejected_as_unmeasured` verifies entity expansion and external entities cannot enter measured evidence.

## IR-609 — comparable campaigns and the evaluation report

Authority: IR-609 under IR-605, ADR-042 and the general-tools evaluation contract. Additive to the IR-598 scorer, which is reused unchanged.

- Preflight (`evaluation/campaign_report.py`): stale or missing lock, draft labels (counted), incomplete manifests, duplicate run/case/Conversation ids, unequal corpus/model/data-policy/limits, wrong engine or protocol per arm, two arms on one implementation, fewer than three repetitions (counted). Tests: `test_campaign_report.py` preflight section.
- Smoke and spend: per-operation physical spend, unreported usage at reservation and never zero, cold/warm apart, price basis, cap coverage. Tests: spend and smoke sections, hand-worked figures.
- Report: per-repetition scores with own denominators, family `INCONCLUSIVE`/`unreviewed`, breach and unreviewed-family blocking, unmeasured arms and IR-608 scenarios, examples, exclusions, approvals, proxy-corpus statement; counted reports refuse a lowered family minimum. Tests: report section and `test_campaign_command.py`.
- Held-out gate: `eval_replay_conversations --held-out` needs `locked_at` and frozen thresholds (now written into every lock), and recorded approvals for real providers. Tests: `test_campaign_command.py`.

**Executed evidence (2026-10-30, Windows, local Postgres):** `python -m pytest apps/ai/evaluation/tests/test_campaign_report.py apps/ai/evaluation/tests/test_campaign_command.py`: 52 passed. `flake8` on the touched modules: clean. `python manage.py eval_campaign --smoke <fixture smoke run> --case-cost-usd 0.1 --campaign-cost-usd 2 --planned-cases 20` ran from the CLI and reported `not ready` (fake providers, one turn with missing usage), as expected. The full backend suite's failures were compared file by file against a clean `origin/main` worktree: none is added by this change. Pre-existing failures come from the local `.env` (inference profiles), `core.autocrlf` (proxy audit digest) and an uncommitted engine switch in the author's checkout. No campaign was run and no quality claim is made.


## IR-679 — Accepted review workspace contract

**Design basis.** Amendment proposed 2026-10-30 and accepted 2026-10-31 by Lee Jasmin Adolfo to ADR-032 §7/§10 and screen spec 16 §4, indexed in the ADR README. Covers Conversation/Readiness, three regions, width clamps and container breakpoints, capability-driven actions, and review privacy. Existing workflow rules remain in force; ADR-047 is separate.

**Verification.** Documentation checked against IR-679 and IR-678 acceptance criteria and the committed prototype; relative links checked and `git diff --check` passed. No runtime behavior or requirements implementation is claimed. Lee Jasmin Adolfo accepted the amendment and authorized merge on 2026-10-31 in the implementation chat. Acceptance is recorded in ADR-032 and on PR #363. The conflict resolution preserves both IR-679 and IR-680 evidence entries.


## IR-680 — Review workspace composition boundary

**Basis and scope.** IR-678/IR-680, ADR-032 §10 and Paper View spec 16 §4. Current `main` already composed the reader, status/timeline and action bar in `ReviewSection`; this pure refactor moves that boundary to `ReviewWorkspace`. PaperViewPage still owns the record fetch, reader, section selection and existing callbacks, passing the viewer's server-derived capabilities. ReviewTimeline keeps its existing tracker read. The Overview-only routing tracker stays in Overview. No workflow, permission or visible layout changes; the three-column workspace remains IR-681.

**Executed evidence (2026-10-30).** Frontend `npm test -- --maxWorkers=2`: **110 files, 1402 tests passed**, with existing tests unchanged, including PaperViewPage, ReviewActionBar, ReviewStatus, ReviewTimeline and ReviewRoutingTracker. The initial targeted run passed 172 tests but failed to spawn its last Windows forks worker (`spawn UNKNOWN`); the complete two-worker rerun passed without that error. `npm run build` passed TypeScript and Vite production build. `npm run lint` passed with zero errors and six existing warnings. `git diff --check` passed. No new behavior was introduced, so no new test suite was added for the rename.

**Visual comparison.** [Before/after evidence](evidence/IR-680/README.md) for an Adviser and ITSO reviewer in Review mode: both pairs have identical PNG SHA-256 hashes and the same record/manuscript/tracker requests. Real app and PDF reader, synthetic API fixtures, 1600×1000 viewport. This establishes the requested wide-layout regression comparison, not every workflow state.

**Review.** Independent Standards and Spec reviews against baseline `c6b6c1e` found no defects. Lee Jasmin Adolfo merged PR #364 on 2026-10-31 (Manila); all four CI checks passed. The implementation chat also explicitly authorizes merge and cleanup. Merge commit: `9933202dd69cf4466b8ef0530486b7334a3f92f7`.
